After three minutes of a task that encouraged head movement, spatial accuracy declined by 0.59 cm, while the gaze center at each target shifted by a mean distance of only 0.37 cm. On the validation block, the offset was 0.30 cm larger for men than for women. Age, region of residence, face luminance, and Michelson contrast showed no reliable relationship with spatial accuracy. A higher eye aspect ratio, a more open eye, was associated with a smaller offset. Wearing glasses decreased spatial accuracy on every validation block. In a comparison between a random subsample ($N = 101$) and a separate sample tested with the open-source tracker WebGazer ($N = 101$) using an identical protocol, Chiasm showed better spatial accuracy (2.18 vs. 4.51 cm) and spatial precision (SD; 1.01 vs. 1.55 cm). WebGazer showed better sample-to-sample precision (RMS-S2S; 0.79 vs. 1.26 cm), which likely reflects conservative, over-smoothed estimates rather than superior stability. These results suggest that Chiasm is narrowing the gap between webcam and hardware-based eye tracking, making large-scale gaze research "in the wild" a reality.

</details>


### [105] [PATTS: Parallel-Aware Test-Time Scaling for Thought-Image Generation in Multimodal Discrete Diffusion LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04948)
*Yasong Dai,Zeeshan Hayder,David Ahmedt-Aristizabal,Hongdong Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 PATTS，一种面向并行多模态离散扩散模型的测试时扩展框架，通过在生成轨迹中对中间"思考-图像"状态进行跨模态验证搜索，显著提升 GenEval 表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图像生成的 TTS 方法通常只对已完成图像或图像中心的中间状态进行排序，忽略推理 token 与图像 token 联合解码的轨迹结构；且诊断发现 CLIP 式图文分数与提示内组合正确性对齐较弱，验证器选择与 oracle 选择间存在较大差距。

Method: 将 TTS 扩展到并行多模态离散扩散语言模型，在单一轨迹中联合解码推理与图像 token；提出 PATTS，利用跨模态验证信号搜索中间 thought-image 状态，并引入由模型内部轨迹诊断导出的状态相关可靠性权重，调节外部语义验证在生成过程中的影响强度。

Result: 在 MMaDA-Parallel 上固定 N=32 的评测中，PATTS 的 GenEval 得分为 0.844，高于无 TTS 的 0.628、Best-of-N 的 0.787 以及 FKD 的 0.810。

Conclusion: 可靠性感知的轨迹验证可有效改进并行 thought-image 协同生成下的测试时选择；结果限于所评估的搜索预算，代码已开源。

Abstract: Test-time scaling (TTS) improves generative models by allocating additional inference-time computation to sampling, search, and verification. Existing image generation approaches, however, typically rank completed images or image-centric intermediate states. We study TTS for parallel multimodal discrete diffusion language models, in which reasoning tokens and image tokens are decoded jointly within a single generation trajectory. We first diagnose a limitation of conventional final-image selection: on GenEval, a CLIP-style image-prompt score is weakly aligned with within-prompt compositional correctness, leaving substantial headroom between verifier selection and oracle selection. We then propose PATTS, a novel parallel-aware TTS framework that searches over intermediate thought-image states using cross-modal verifier signals. PATTS uses a state-dependent reliability weight, derived from model-internal trajectory diagnostics, to modulate the influence of external semantic verification during generation. In the fixed N=32 evaluation on MMaDA-Parallel, the PATTS reaches a GenEval score of 0.844, compared with 0.628 without TTS, 0.787 for Best-of-N, and 0.810 for FKD. These results show that reliability-aware trajectory verification can improve test-time selection for parallel thought-image co-generation under the evaluated search budget. Our code release is at https://github.com/Ringo-Star/PATTS.git

</details>


### [106] [DPAGait: Dynamic Pose-Knowledge-Aware Network for Robust Gait Recognition in the Wild](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04950)
*Zenan Lin,Junduan Huang,Kun Liu,M. Saad Shakeel,Wenxiong Kang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出动态姿态解析增强的轮廓步态表征（DPPS）与双分支姿态知识感知步态网络，用于野外鲁棒步态识别。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有步态识别方法主要依赖二值轮廓图像，信息熵低且未充分利用步态序列中的动态信息，难以应对换装、携带物、视角及动态环境变化，导致野外场景下精度与鲁棒性显著下降。

Method: 1) 提出DPPS表征范式，将姿态-解析线索与轮廓融合，提升信息熵并捕捉细粒度行为线索；2) 构建双分支Dynamic Pose-Knowledge-Aware Gait网络：2D动态姿态感知分支从DPPS中提取姿态增强特征，3D SMPL分支利用SMPL模型捕获丰富的三维动态姿态信息；3) 融合两分支特征进行识别。

Result: 在四个具有挑战性的野外步态数据集上验证了DPPS表征的有效性与泛化性；完整框架在SUSTech1K、CCPG、Gait3D（SMPL信息可得或可一致估计）上取得有竞争力的性能。

Conclusion: 融合姿态-解析线索与轮廓的DPPS表征结合2D/3D双分支姿态知识感知框架，可有效提升野外步态识别的准确性与鲁棒性。

Abstract: Gait biometrics aims at identity recognition by appearance or pose features captured during unconstrained human walking. Existing methods predominantly rely on binary silhouette images, inherently characterized by low information entropy, and fail to fully utilize the dynamic information in gait sequences, limiting their ability to handle cross-time variations effectively. Consequently, these methods encounter significant challenges in the wild. They are susceptible to variations in clothing, carried objects, and viewpoints, as well as dynamic environmental conditions, all of which lead to substantial degradation in recognition accuracy and robustness. To overcome these challenges, we propose a pose-knowledge-aware approach specifically designed for robust gait recognition in the wild. First, we propose a novel Dynamic Pose-Parsing enhanced Silhouette gait representation paradigm that substantially enhances the information entropy and effectively captures fine-grained behavioral cues by integrating pose-parsing cues with traditional silhouettes. Subsequently, we develop a dual-branch Dynamic Pose-Knowledge-Aware Gait network. The framework consists of two complementary branches: (1) a 2D dynamic pose-aware branch that extracts pose-enhanced features from our proposed DPPS representations, and (2) a 3D Skinned Multi-Person Linear branch that leverages the 3D SMPL model to capture rich and comprehensive 3D dynamic pose information. Finally, the features are integrated to facilitate accurate and robust gait recognition. Extensive experiments on four challenging in-the-wild gait datasets demonstrate the effectiveness and generalizability of the proposed DPPS representation. The complete framework is further evaluated on SUSTech1K, CCPG, and Gait3D, where SMPL information is available or can be consistently estimated, achieving competitive performance under diverse real-world conditions.

</details>


### [107] [Lightweight graph transformers for clutter and target classification in automotive radar](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04959)
*Adrian Gheorghiu,Tunc Alkanat,Ashish Pandharipande*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出轻量级微型图Transformer网络TGTNet，用于车载雷达场景中杂波与静止/运动目标的分类；在RadarScenes上精度与召回接近SOTA，而模型规模小1–2个数量级、推理显著更快。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 雷达是自动驾驶场景感知的核心传感器，但杂波（非真实运动目标的检测）普遍存在，会损害目标检测与分类性能并影响后续驾驶决策，因此需有效区分杂波与真实目标；同时现有方法模型体积大、推理慢，难以满足车载实时部署需求。

Method: 构建轻量化的tiny graph transformer network（TGTNet）模型，将雷达点云检测建模为图结构并利用图Transformer进行特征交互与分类，对场景中的杂波、静止目标与运动目标进行判别；通过微型化网络设计压缩参数与计算量。

Result: 在公开数据集RadarScenes上评估，TGTNet在precision与recall上与SOTA模型相当，模型规模低1–2个数量级，且推理速度显著更快。

Conclusion: TGTNet在保持杂波分类精度竞争力的同时大幅降低模型规模与推理延迟，为实现车载雷达场景感知中的高效、可部署杂波抑制提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Radar is a core sensor modality for scene perception to achieve higher levels of autonomy in automotive driving. A common occurrence in automotive radars is clutter - detections of non-existent moving objects, that can adversely impact target detection and classification performance and subsequent driving actions. We propose lightweight tiny graph transformer network (TGTNet) models for classifying clutter from stationary and moving targets in the scene. Performance evaluation on the public RadarScenes show that our proposed TGTNet models achieve similar classification performance in precision and recall metrics in comparison to state of the art models, with one to two orders of magnitude lower model size and significantly faster inference.

</details>


### [108] [An Agentic Framework for Quality-Aware and Scenario-Adaptive Image Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04960)
*Zhenghao Chen,Lebin Zhou,Zicong Chen,Naifu Xue,Jiahao Li,Bin Li,Dong Xu,Yan Lu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PICA：多智能体框架，将现有编解码器适配到多样用户需求与退化输入；并提出Agentic ROI Coding（AROIC）实现编解码器无关的区域自适应编码，配合SGQR评测策略，在H.266/VVC与Cheng2020上一致提升压缩性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图像编解码器存在两大局限：(1) 多为单一目标优化，压缩目标变化时需大量重新开发；(2) 通常假设输入干净，而传感器图像常含退化，改变率-质量行为。因此需要一种能适配多样需求与退化输入、且不依赖特定编解码器架构的通用方案。

Method: 提出PICA多智能体框架，含四个协作智能体：Perception（解析用户意图与图像内容）、Improvement（联合考虑码率与重建质量以引导编码前复原）、Coding（从全局到区域细化质量控制设置）、After-coding improvement（仅以图像质量为引导，处理残余退化与压缩伪影）。为实现编解码器无关的区域适配，提出AROIC，将编解码器视为黑盒，使智能体能自动调度、执行、评估并反思ROI编码计划，无需架构修改或编解码器专用重训练。四智能体与Scenario-Guided Quality-Rate（SGQR）评测策略集成，实现跨异构编解码器的场景自适应压缩。

Result: 在Kodak、CLIC2020、HEVC数据集的干净、噪声、模糊图像上，覆盖标准、感知与机器视觉三类压缩场景的实验表明，PICA一致提升传统H.266/VVC与神经Cheng2020编解码器的压缩性能，并优于对比方法。

Conclusion: PICA通过多智能体协作与黑盒式ROI编码，实现了编解码器无关、面向场景与退化输入的自适应压缩，可即插即用地增强现有编解码器，具备良好的通用性与实用性。

Abstract: Despite decades of progress, image codecs remain difficult to adapt to diverse real-world compression scenarios for two main reasons. First, most image codecs are optimized for a specific objective and require substantial redevelopment when the compression goal changes. Second, they typically assume clean inputs, whereas sensor-captured images often contain degradations that alter rate-quality behavior. To address these challenges, we propose Practical Image Coding Agents (PICA), a multi-agent framework that adapts existing codecs to diverse user requirements and potentially degraded inputs. PICA consists of four collaborative agents: a Perception agent that interprets user intent and analyzes image content, an Improvement agent that jointly considers bitrate and reconstruction quality to guide pre-coding restoration, a Coding agent that refines quality-control settings from global to regional levels, and an After-coding improvement agent that addresses residual degradations and compression-induced artifacts through restoration guided solely by image quality. To enable codec-agnostic regional adaptation, we introduce Agentic ROI Coding (AROIC), which treats the codec as a black box and allows the agent to automatically schedule, execute, evaluate, and reflect ROI coding plans without architectural modifications or codec-specific retraining. We integrate these four agents with a Scenario-Guided Quality-Rate (SGQR) evaluation strategy to enable more effective scenario-adaptive compression across heterogeneous codecs. Comprehensive experiments on clean, noisy, and blurred images across standard, perceptual, and machine-vision compression scenarios demonstrate that PICA consistently enhances the compression performance of both the conventional H.266/VVC codec and the neural Cheng2020 codec, outperforming competing compression methods on the Kodak, CLIC2020, and HEVC datasets.

</details>


### [109] [ProAlign: Progressive Positional and Prototype-Guided Alignment for Aerial-Ground Person Re-Identification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04980)
*Qiao Li,Jing Chen,Cong Wu,Kun He,Ruiying Du,Lei Tan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出ProAlign网络，通过逐层渐进位置嵌入与视角感知原型对比学习解决AG-ReID中的位置漂移与跨视角语义不一致问题，在CARGO等基准上取得SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AG-ReID需匹配无人机与地面摄像头行人图像，存在严重视角差异、遮挡与域间差距；现有方法还忽视两个结构性问题：层次特征提取中的累积位置漂移，以及跨视角特征分布的语义不一致。

Method: ProAlign含两模块：LPPE在Transformer各层进行自适应位置校准，浅层用条件位置编码缓解局部空间畸变，深层引入可学习静态位置嵌入强化全局语义先验；PCL为每个身份维护空中与地面视角专属原型，并通过双原型对比目标强制跨视角语义一致性。

Result: 在CARGO及两个真实场景AG-ReID基准上验证有效性；CARGO上取得整体检索SOTA，Rank-1提升1.60%，mAP提升5.01%（相对最强对比方法）。

Conclusion: 渐进式位置校准与双原型对比学习可有效缓解位置漂移和跨视角语义不一致，显著提升AG-ReID检索性能。

Abstract: Aerial-Ground Person Re-Identification (AG-ReID) aims to match pedestrian images captured by unmanned aerial vehicles (UAVs) and ground-based surveillance cameras. The task remains highly challenging due to severe viewpoint discrepancies, frequent occlusions, and substantial domain gaps between aerial and ground imagery. Beyond these observable factors, existing methods often overlook two fundamental structural issues: cumulative positional drift during hierarchical feature extraction and semantic inconsistency across cross-view feature distributions. To address these challenges, we propose ProAlign, a Progressive Positional and Prototype-Guided Alignment Network for AG-ReID. ProAlign comprises two key components: a Layer-wise Progressive Positional Embedding (LPPE) module and a View-Aware Prototype Contrastive Learning (PCL) module. LPPE performs adaptive positional calibration throughout transformer layers by employing conditional positional encoding in shallow layers to mitigate local spatial distortions, while introducing learnable static positional embeddings in deeper layers to reinforce global semantic priors. Meanwhile, PCL maintains view-specific aerial and ground prototypes for each identity and enforces cross-view semantic consistency via a dual-prototype contrastive objective. Extensive experiments on CARGO and two real-world AG-ReID benchmarks demonstrate the effectiveness of ProAlign, achieving state-of-the-art overall retrieval performance on CARGO with improvements of 1.60% in Rank-1 accuracy and 5.01% in mAP over the strongest competing methods.

</details>


### [110] [ScoreDial: Precise Control of Visual Attributes in Image Generation via Regressor Guidance from Limited Human Ratings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05004)
*Jianbo Tang,Keita Ishii,Hiroki Sakamoto,Manabu Kikuchi,Katsuma Kitamura,Chiho Semba,Kenta Nishiyama,Teppei Mori,Yukihiro Kiwaki,Takeo Kato*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ScoreDial 通过拟合人类评分回归器 R 并用误差自适应引导在采样中反复降低 |R(x)−c|，实现严格的"请求值控制"，在四个属性上达到 R-MAE≤0.013、Spearman ρ=0.980。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 连续引导（continuous steering）只能沿属性方向移动，但"请求值控制"要求生成图像仅在少量人类评分下精确达到指定分数 c，这一更严格的目标尚未被现有方法解决，需要兼顾精确性与真实属性变化而非仅对齐代理评分器。

Method: 用 100–479 张人类评分图像拟合可微回归器 R；固定 R 后在其输出尺度上定义请求值 c，结合学习到的目标条件化（target conditioning）与误差自适应引导（error-adaptive guidance），在采样过程中迭代减小 |R(x)−c|。四个属性（轮胎抗滑性、室内视觉复杂度、食物食用欲望、肥皂半透明度）共用一套采样器设置；并引入独立审计回归器与盲评人类研究，以分离"精确分数校正"与"真实属性位移"。

Result: 四个属性上 R-MAE 最多 0.013（范围宽度 0.8），Spearman ρ=0.980（评估协议下最大值）；盲评中轮胎与肥皂的平均人类评分随 c 线性上升，R²=0.665 与 0.786；匹配扫描的强制选择中，84% 以上判断认为 ScoreDial 的进展更清晰。但 ScoreDial 在独立审计器上的位移为所有方法中最大，MPGD-flow 虽也达到 R 下的目标值却位移更小；更强的 MPGD-flow 设置无法弥合该差距。移除目标条件化后 R-MAE 仍低但审计器位移减半以上。

Conclusion: ScoreDial 能在统一设置下精确达到请求分数，验证了目标条件化与误差自适应引导的有效性；同时揭示仅满足评分器的低 R-MAE 并不等价于真实属性变化，需借助独立审计器与人类评估加以区分，二者共同构成对该类方法的必要检验。

Abstract: Requested-value control is stricter than continuous steering: given $c$ on a human-rating scale, the generated image should attain that value from only limited ratings. We present ScoreDial, which fits a differentiable regressor $R$ to 100-479 human-rated images; once $R$ is fixed, a requested value $c$ is defined on its output scale, and learned target conditioning is paired with error-adaptive guidance that repeatedly reduces $|R(\mathbf{x})-c|$ during sampling. Across tire slip resistance, interior visual complexity, food desire-to-eat, and soap translucency, using one set of sampler settings shared across all four, ScoreDial reaches requested values and achieves, on all four attributes, $R$-MAE at most 0.013 over a range of width 0.8 and Spearman $ρ=0.980$, the maximum attainable under our evaluation protocol. Because $R$ also drives sampling, we evaluate agreement with $R$ separately from matched sweeps, an independent auditor fitted to the same ratings on a backbone none of the deployed regressors uses, and blinded human judgments. MPGD-flow also reaches the requested values under $R$, but ScoreDial has larger auditor displacement on every attribute, the largest among all compared methods, and a stronger MPGD-flow setting gives up target agreement without closing this gap. On every attribute, removing target conditioning leaves low $R$-MAE but more than halves auditor displacement, separating precise score correction from attribute movement. Blinded rating studies with 15 raters per attribute show that mean human ratings track $c$ on tire and soap, with linear $R^2=0.665$ and $R^2=0.786$, and in forced-choice comparisons of matched sweeps, raters chose ScoreDial's as the clearer progression in at least 84% of judgments against every baseline.

</details>


### [111] [Learned Smartphone ISP on Mobile GPUs: Mobile AI 2026 Challenge Report](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05008)
*Andrey Ignatov,Georgy Perevozchikov,Radu Timofte,Yuhong He,Yuxuan Qiu,Tianyu Hao,Ruoqi Li,Yueqi Yang,Chaoyu Feng,Dongqing Zou,Lei Lei,Na Jiang,Yongrui Zhang,Hailong Yan,Xiangtao Zhang,Ao Li,Le Zhang,Andrei Arhire,Jing Fang,XingLin Xie,YiChen Lu,Haibo Xu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文介绍Mobile AI挑战赛中的一项任务：在Qualcomm与Arm移动GPU上，以低于100ms的延迟将RAW Bayer数据重建为高质量RGB照片。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能手机相机画质日益重要，计算摄影与ISP算法需求上升，但高效深度学习模型需同时兼顾性能与受限移动硬件上的可部署性。

Method: 构建大规模Fujifilm UltraISP数据集（Sony IMX586 Quad Bayer传感器RAW与FujiFilm GFX100 102MP中画幅相机的RAW-RGB配对），参赛者在Snapdragon 8 Elite Gen 5（Adreno）与Dimensity 9500（Mali）平台上设计并评测快速RAW-to-RGB重建模型。

Result: 顶级方案可在两个平台上于100毫秒内从RAW数据重建高质量5MP RGB图像。

Conclusion: 论文系统描述了挑战赛中开发的各类模型，展示了移动端实时RAW-to-RGB计算摄影的可行性与高效轻量模型设计方向。

Abstract: With the increasing importance of mobile camera quality in modern smartphones, growing attention has been directed toward computational photography and advanced image signal processing (ISP) algorithms aimed at enhancing various perceptual attributes of captured images. This creates a need for efficient deep learning models for this problem that are not only performant but can also be deployed on constrained mobile hardware. In this Mobile AI challenge, we address this problem and propose the participants to design fast solutions that can reconstruct RGB photos from RAW Bayer camera data while running on popular smartphone GPUs from Qualcomm and Arm. For this, the participants were provided with a large-scale Fujifilm UltraISP dataset consisting of RAW-RGB image pairs captured with the Sony IMX586 Quad Bayer mobile sensor and a professional 102MP medium format FujiFilm GFX100 camera. The runtime of all models was evaluated on the Snapdragon 8 Elite Gen 5 and Dimensity 9500 chipsets with the Adreno and Mali GPUs, respectively. Top solutions proposed in this challenge are able to reconstruct high-quality 5MP RGB image from raw data on both platforms under 100 milliseconds. A comprehensive description of the models developed in the challenge is provided in this paper.

</details>


### [112] [Can saliency maps flag chest radiograph classifier errors at a new hospital? An external validation study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05011)
*Aashish Chapagai,Angad Singh Gurtatta,Erik Cambria*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究在训练医院与两家外部医院共评估约30万次决策，发现Grad-CAM的解剖富集度无法超越模型置信度用于检测个体错误，且在卷积网络上富集度与正确性的关系在不同医院间反转，建议以置信度而非显著性图作为部署监测依据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 显著性图（saliency maps）常被当作医学图像分类器的安全保障，用以揭示个体错误；但其能否在新医院的个体决策上揭示错误，此前基本未经检验。

Method: 在NIH ChestX-ray14上训练CNN与Vision Transformer；在训练医院及美国、越南两家外部站点，以放射科医师共识标注为金标准，每模型评估303,768次决策。以解剖富集度（归因在分割胸腔区域内面积归一化的集中程度）对每张解释打分，并检验其能否在模型置信度之外额外检测错误。

Result: Grad-CAM 定位病灶的比例低于放射科医师（命中率最高0.575 vs 0.888）；将富集度加入在训练医院校准的置信度错误检测器后，AUC提升从未超过0.011；对卷积网络，富集度与正确性的关系在医院间发生反转，导致迁移后的检测器性能下降。

Conclusion: 显著性图不提供超越置信度的个体错误检测增益，且跨院迁移不稳定；部署监测应以模型置信度而非saliency为基础。

Abstract: Saliency maps are offered as a safeguard for medical image classifiers, but whether they reveal individual errors at a new hospital remains largely untested. We trained a convolutional network and a vision transformer on NIH ChestX-ray14 and evaluated 303,768 decisions per model at the training hospital and at two external sites, in the USA and Vietnam, with radiologist-consensus labels. We scored each explanation by its anatomical enrichment, the area-normalized concentration of attribution in a segmented thoracic region, and tested whether it detects errors beyond the model's confidence. Grad-CAM localized findings less often than radiologists (hit rate at most 0.575 versus 0.888). Adding enrichment to a confidence-based error detector calibrated at the training hospital never raised its area under the curve by more than 0.011, and for the convolutional network the relation between enrichment and correctness reversed between hospitals, degrading the transferred detector. Model confidence, not saliency, should anchor deployment monitoring.

</details>


### [113] [RGB Photo Enhancement with Deep Learning on Smartphone GPUs: Mobile AI 2026 Challenge Report](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05024)
*Andrey Ignatov,Georgy Perevozchikov,Radu Timofte,Dong Zhang,Zhao Ran,Yang Zhang,Xiaochen Li,Shichang Ju,Yiqin Cao,Yang Lu,Nguyen Nhu Tinh Anh,To Thanh Dat,Nguyen Ngoc Ky Duyen,Nguyen Trong Nghia,Bui Minh Hieu,Qiuhai Yan,Sunzhe Yang,Kang Chen,Tingting Wang,Faming Fang,Runhua Deng,Zhifeng Wang,Long Peng,Aiwen Jiang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文是Mobile AI挑战赛中移动端RGB照片增强赛道的总结报告，要求参赛者在Qualcomm与Arm的智能手机GPU上实现高效的RGB图像综合增强。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 移动设备拍摄及其它来源图像的质量提升需要端侧RGB照片增强，而可部署于受限移动硬件的高性能深度学习模型仍显不足。

Method: 基于大规模DPED数据集（旧款iPhone 3GS与专业Canon 70D单反拍摄的RGB图像对）训练与评测；运行时间在Snapdragon 8 Elite Gen 5（Adreno GPU）与Dimensity 9500（Mali GPU）上测量。

Result: 挑战赛中的最优方案可在两个平台上于300毫秒内完成Full HD分辨率RGB图像增强。

Conclusion: 论文全面描述了挑战赛中开发的各参赛模型，为端侧高效RGB照片增强提供了方案参考。

Abstract: On-device RGB photo enhancement is a popular task that is needed for quality improvement of photos captured with mobile devices as well as for retouching of images acquired through other sources. This creates a need for efficient deep learning models for this problem that are not only performant but can also be deployed on constrained mobile hardware. In this Mobile AI challenge, we address this problem and propose participants to design fast solutions that can perform comprehensive enhancement of RGB photos while running on popular smartphone GPUs from Qualcomm and Arm. For this, the participants were provided with a large-scale DPED dataset consisting of RGB image pairs captured with an old iPhone 3GS smartphone and a professional Canon 70D DSLR camera. The runtime of all models was evaluated on the Snapdragon 8 Elite Gen 5 and Dimensity 9500 chipsets with the Adreno and Mali GPUs, respectively. Top solutions proposed in this challenge are able to enhance Full HD resolution RGB images on both platforms under 300 milliseconds. A comprehensive description of the models developed in the challenge is provided in this paper.

</details>


### [114] [On the Learnability of Footwear Robustness: Footstep Recognition Under Unseen Conditions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05049)
*Robyn Larracy,Erik Scheme*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究提出BiStepNet架构，用于在用户注册时固定鞋类、识别时鞋类任意的困难场景下，利用足底压力/力敏步道实现跨鞋类步态生物识别，并证明鞋类引起的偏移具有可学习的共享结构，可零样本迁移到未见用户与鞋款。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 足底压力式步态识别便捷、被动，但鞋类变化同时影响足-传感器接口与步态力学，是实用化的一大障碍。已有方法难以应对注册鞋类固定、测试鞋类任意且未见的场景，因此需要建模并去除鞋类引起的域偏移。

Method: 基于StepUP-P150数据集；提出BiStepNet，将空间身份线索与时间身份线索分解为可独立训练的双流结构；以紧凑3D时空模型为基线进行对比；通过改变训练集中鞋类的数量与多样性，考察跨鞋类识别性能的扩展规律；并借助分解式设计，引入仅有部分模态重叠的辅助步态数据集以增强鲁棒性。

Result: BiStepNet在跨鞋类识别上优于紧凑3D时空基线；跨鞋类性能随训练集鞋类表示的量与多样性系统性提升，且增益可迁移至训练中未直接出现的鞋款；部分模态重叠的辅助步态数据可进一步提升对未见鞋类的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 鞋类诱发的偏移虽大且个体间异质，但包含可从训练人群学习并零样本迁移到未见用户和鞋款的共享结构；分解式空间-时间架构是应对跨鞋类步态识别的有效设计，且能灵活融合部分重叠的辅助数据。

Abstract: Footstep patterns, captured using pressure or force-sensitive walkways during natural walking, offer a convenient and passive approach to biometric recognition. However, footwear variability poses a major challenge to practical deployment, as it impacts both the foot-sensor interface and the user's underlying gait mechanics. This study considers the particularly difficult scenario where users enroll under one fixed footwear condition but must later be recognized in arbitrary, unseen footwear. Using the StepUP-P150 dataset, we show that footwear-induced shift, while substantial and heterogeneous across individuals, contains shared structure that can be learned from a training population and transferred, zero-shot, to previously unseen users and footwear styles. We do this using BiStepNet, a novel architecture that decomposes spatial and temporal identity cues into independently trainable streams and outperforms compact 3D spatiotemporal baselines for cross-footwear recognition. We demonstrate that cross-footwear recognition performance scales systematically with the volume and diversity of footwear representation in the training set, with gains transferring to footwear styles not directly observed during training. Moreover, we show that auxiliary gait datasets with only partial modality overlap (incorporated through the architecture's decomposed design) can be leveraged to further improve robustness to unseen footwear conditions.

</details>


### [115] [FloMo: 3D Scene Flow as a World Action Model Intermediate for Learning from Human Video](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05062)
*Jeremy Collins,Namra Patel,Ayush Agarwal,Shitij Govil,Animesh Garg*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FloMo将预训练视频生成模型改造为3D场景流（scene flow）与动作token的联合预测模型，证明稠密3D运动是视频预训练向机器人动作迁移的最佳中介：以运动预测替代视频预测可提升样本效率与分布外泛化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预训练视频生成模型虽能将互联网规模的动先验蒸馏进机器人策略，但完整视频对动作预测而言是冗余信号。作者主张：稠密3D运动才是视频预训练迁移到机器人动作的介质，且预测运动而非视频能为动作预测带来更好的样本效率。

Method: 构建5B参数视频生成模型FloMo，微调后同时预测3D场景流与动作token；将场景流渲染为RGB并编码进与视频相同的潜空间，使预训练视频主干可高效适配到3D场景流预测；在无动作人类视频与带动作标签的机器人遥操作数据上联合训练。

Result: 模型受益于人类视频预训练，并泛化到未见物体、运动基元与任务。消融显示：用视频预测目标替换运动预测会降低分布外泛化；在运动目标之外额外加入视频预测目标反而降低策略成功率；三者联合预测时，动作token对运动的注意力远高于对视频的注意力，说明预测视频浪费模型容量。

Conclusion: 运动是视频预训练向动作迁移的有效中介；预测稠密3D运动比预测视频更契合机器人动作学习，兼顾样本效率与泛化能力。

Abstract: Pretrained video generation models distill internet-scale motion priors into robot policies, but full video is a redundant signal for action prediction. We argue that dense 3D motion is the medium through which video pretraining best transfers to robot actions, and that predicting motion rather than video affords better sample-efficiency for action prediction. FloMo is a 5B video-generation model fine-tuned to predict 3D scene-flow and action tokens. Rendering scene flow as RGB and encoding it into the same latent space as video enables a pretrained video generation backbone to efficiently adapt to predict 3D scene flow. Co-trained on action-free human video and action-labeled robot teleoperation data, our model benefits from human-video pretraining and generalizes to unseen objects, motion primitives, and tasks. Replacing motion prediction with a video prediction objective degrades out-of-distribution generalization, while adding a video prediction target alongside motion lowers policy success rather than improving it. When motion, video, and action are predicted jointly, action tokens attend far more to motion than to video, suggesting that predicting video wastes model capacity. Together, these results indicate that motion effectively mediates the transfer from video pretraining to action. More information can be found at flomo-wam.github.io.

</details>


### [116] [VIGOR: Visual Gain Ordering for Hallucination Mitigation in Multimodal Discrete Diffusion Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05066)
*Junzhe Chen,Tian Qin,Eugenie Shi,Tianshu Zhang,Qiang Ju,Lijie Wen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出免训练、即插即用的推理策略VIGOR，通过对掩码位置进行反事实视觉证据重排序，缓解多模态离散扩散语言模型的幻觉问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态离散扩散语言模型（dLLM）通过迭代掩码预测生成响应，但存在幻觉：输出虽符合语言先验却缺乏图像依据。作者认为幻觉不仅是token预测问题，更是"过早提交"问题——迭代去噪中部分掩码位置因置信度更多由语言先验驱动而非视觉证据而过早解掩，进而偏差后续生成。

Method: 提出Visual Gain Ordering（VIGOR）：在每个去噪步，分别计算候选token在原始图像条件下的置信度与额外视觉消融（visual-ablation）条件下的置信度，比较二者差异，优先解掩那些预测更强烈受图像支持的位置，从而实现反事实视觉证据驱动的掩码位置重排序。该方法免训练且即插即用。

Result: 在LLaDA-V与Lumina-DiMOO上实验表明，VIGOR在各基准上一致降低幻觉并提升多模态推理性能。代码已开源。

Conclusion: 将幻觉归因于迭代去噪中的过早提交，并以反事实视觉增益作为解掩顺序依据，可有效提升多模态dLLM的视觉接地性与推理表现。

Abstract: Multimodal discrete diffusion language models (dLLMs) generate responses through iterative mask prediction and offer a promising alternative to autoregressive large vision-language models. However, they still suffer from hallucination, producing outputs that are plausible under language priors but insufficiently grounded in the image. We argue that hallucination in multimodal dLLMs is not only a token prediction problem, but also a premature commitment problem: during iterative denoising, some masked positions are unmasked too early because their confidence is driven more by language priors than by visual evidence, and these early commitments can bias later generation. To address this issue, we propose Visual Gain Ordering (VIGOR), a training-free and plug-and-play inference strategy that reorders masked positions according to counterfactual visual evidence. At each denoising step, VIGOR compares the confidence of each candidate token under the original image-conditioned pass and an additional visual-ablation pass, and prioritizes positions whose predictions are more strongly supported by the image. Experiments on LLaDA-V and Lumina-DiMOO show that VIGOR consistently reduces hallucination and improves multimodal reasoning performance across benchmarks. Code is available at https://github.com/THU-BPM/Vigor.

</details>


### [117] [Manifold Embedding of Deep Image Features for Image Matching via Neural Adjoint Maps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05083)
*Nafie El Amrani,Moritz Heep,Florian Bernard*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出基于泛函映射（functional maps）的零样本图像语义对应方法NAMI，利用Galerkin有限元法从深度特征流形嵌入中推导离散Laplace-Beltrami算子，并借助神经伴随映射表示非线性映射，在多个稠密对应基准上达到零样本SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基础模型特征虽推动了图像对应，但基于最近邻的匹配缺乏足够正则化，稠密对应下噪声尤为严重，需要更强正则化与低维线性表示框架。

Method: 将图像对应转化为泛函映射问题；由于矩形图像网格本身缺乏有意义的几何结构，采用Galerkin有限元法，基于深度特征的流形嵌入构造离散Laplace-Beltrami算子（LBO），从而赋予图像流形结构；并用神经伴随映射（neural adjoint map）实现非线性映射表达。

Result: 在多个真实图像稠密对应基准上，方法在零样本图像对应方法中取得新的state-of-the-art。

Conclusion: 将3D形状匹配中的谱泛函映射范式迁移至图像对应领域是可行且有效的，关键在于为图像网格构造有信息量的流形几何并支持非线性映射。

Abstract: Estimating semantic correspondences between different object instances of similar categories in real-world images is a fundamental challenge in computer vision. While the recent progress in foundation models has significantly advanced solutions for such difficult image correspondence problems, respective methods are often insufficiently regularised. For example, common nearest-neighbour matching with foundation model features often results in noisy correspondences, which is particularly prominent when solving for dense correspondences. In this work, we tackle image matching via functional maps, which have been popularised for 3D shape matching due to their powerful spectral formalism utilising an efficient low-dimensional linear representation. However, functional maps require that domains (in our cases the rectangular image grids) are equipped with an informative and non-trivial geometry. To define a meaningful manifold structure for images, we use Galerkin's method from finite elements to derive a discrete Laplace-Beltrami operator (LBO) based on a manifold embedding of deep image features. Further, we leverage a neural adjoint map that allows to represent non-linear mappings. We demonstrate that our method sets the new state of the art among zero-shot image correspondence methods on multiple dense correspondence benchmarks of real-world images. Our code is publicly available: https://github.com/NafieAmrani/NAMI

</details>


### [118] [Sparse Anchors for Visual Multi-Session Registration in Changing Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05084)
*Yunus Talha Erzurumlu,Niklas Trekel,Saurabh Gupta,Nico Koltermann,Olga Vysotska,Jens Behley,Louis Wiesmann,Cyrill Stachniss*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种稀疏可靠绝对锚点+会话内相对位姿约束的多会话视觉配准方法，将查询轨迹完整配准到参考地图，在HILTI26数据集上1m内召回94.4%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 重复性机器人监测需在同一环境多次访问间定位，但场景的显著视觉与结构变化会削弱持久视觉内容，重复结构又易导致跨会话错误对应，因此部分轨迹可能无法建立可靠跨会话匹配，需要一种能在仅部分可匹配情况下仍完成整体轨迹配准的方案。

Method: 先通过视觉位置识别、局部特征匹配与几何验证生成绝对对齐假设；用序列一致性过滤与共同查询—参考对齐不符的假设，保留一致位姿作为绝对锚点；锚点经高斯特征场上的featuremetric对齐精化；最后将锚点与会话内相对位姿约束联合，在鲁棒位姿图优化中求解完整轨迹配准。

Result: 在HILTI26建筑工地数据集15组跨会话实验中，1m内召回94.4%、5m内100.0%；优于独立图像定位（峰值65.3%与70.7%）以及独立重建SfM几何的直接配准。

Conclusion: 稀疏但可靠的绝对锚点结合会话内相对约束，可在仅部分查询会话存在可靠跨会话对应时，实现准确且完整的轨迹配准。

Abstract: Repeated robotic monitoring requires localization across visits to the same environment, even if the environment changes. Such changes can substantially reduce persistent visual content, and repetitive structures can lead to incorrect cross-session correspondences. In this paper, we address visual multi-session registration of a new data recording (the query session) to a previously reconstructed reference map. Under substantial visual and structural changes, cross-session localization may be possible only for parts of the trajectory. In our approach, we therefore rely on a sparse set of reliable absolute pose constraints and combine them with within-session relative-pose information to register the complete trajectory to the reference map. We first estimate absolute alignment hypotheses through visual place recognition, local feature matching, and geometric verification. We then use sequence consensus to reject hypotheses that are inconsistent with a common query-to-reference alignment and retain the remaining poses as absolute anchors. These anchors are refined through featuremetric alignment against a Gaussian feature field and combined with within-session relative-pose constraints in a robust pose-graph optimization. We evaluate our approach on 15 cross-session experiments from the challenging HILTI26 construction-site dataset, covering substantial scene changes and difficult-to-match environments. Our method achieves 94.4% recall within 1m and 100.0% within 5m, outperforming independent image localization, which peaks at 65.3% and 70.7%, respectively, as well as direct registration of independently reconstructed Structure-from-Motion geometry. Our results show that sparse, reliable absolute anchors can provide accurate and complete trajectory registration even when reliable cross-session correspondences are available only for part of the query session.

</details>


### [119] [Characterizing the vertical continuity of fuel strata based on TLS point clouds Gaussian decomposition and overlap metrics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05102)
*Jesús Torralba,Luis Á. Ruiz,Pablo Crespo-Peremarch,Marina Simó-Martí,Juan Pedro Carbonell-Rivera*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该文提出对TLS点云垂直高度剖面做高斯分解，以连续方式量化垂直可燃物连通性，并在25个固定样地、从样地到约0.5 m²的多尺度上比较几何、概率与重叠指标，发现3–6 m网格尺度最能平衡空间细节与灌木-冠层交互表达的稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 垂直林分结构通过调控林下-冠层可燃物的垂直连通性影响火行为，但业务化可燃物类型模型采用离散类别，只能间接刻画垂直连续性；高密度点云虽可显式表征，但缺乏具体方法。

Method: 基于TLS点云的垂直高度剖面，应用高斯分解刻画几何垂直可燃物连续性；在25个永久样地上开展从样地尺度至约0.5 m²单元的多尺度分析，评估几何指标、概率密度重叠指标及可分性指标（如Jeffries-Matusita距离）对可燃物层的表征能力，并检验点密度与剖面不规则性对结果的影响。

Result: 方法重现了灌木层与乔木层之间的垂直过渡，各分析层级中位Pearson相关系数为0.83–0.96；概率密度重叠与可分性指标提供了垂直连续性的互补描述；3–6 m单元为空间细节与灌木-冠层交互格局一致性之间的较优折中，更细尺度因点密度下降与剖面不规则导致变异性增大；低均方误差不足以作为唯一性能指标。

Conclusion: 高斯分解TLS点云可实现垂直可燃物连续性的显式量化，弥补离散分类系统的不足，为梯状可燃物（ladder fuel）表征及野火风险评估决策支持提供连续垂直连通性指标的改进路径。

Abstract: Vertical forest structure influences fire behavior by regulating understory-to-canopy fuel connectivity, yet operational fuel-type models represent this complexity through discrete classes that only indirectly capture vertical continuity. High-density point clouds offer an opportunity to characterize vertical fuel connectivity in a more explicit and continuous manner, but specific methodological approaches are still required. We applied Gaussian decomposition to vertical height profiles derived from terrestrial laser scanning (TLS) point clouds across 25 permanent forest plots to characterize geometric vertical fuel continuity. A multiscale analysis spanning from the plot scale to ~0.5 $m^2$ units was used to evaluate geometric, probabilistic, and overlap metrics for characterizing fuel strata. The method reproduced the vertical transition between shrub and tree strata, with median Pearson correlations ranging from 0.83 to 0.96 across analysis levels. Probability density overlap metrics and separability indicators, such as the Jeffries-Matusita distance, provided complementary descriptors of vertical continuity. Intermediate spatial scales (3-6 m cells) provided a useful compromise between spatial detail and the consistent representation of shrub-canopy interaction patterns. Finer scales increased variability due to point density reduction and profile irregularity, and low mean squared error values were found insufficient as sole performance indicators. Gaussian decomposition applied to TLS point clouds enables explicit quantification of vertical fuel continuity, a limitation of current discrete classification systems. Continuous vertical connectivity metrics derived from this approach offer a pathway for improving ladder fuel characterization and strengthening decision-making support in wildfire risk assessment.

</details>


### [120] [High-fidelity training-free photovoltaic module mapping from multi-view UAV imagery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05131)
*WenHao Duana,Qi Chena,Penglei Zhang,Ruizhe Deng,Zhiling Guo,Haoran Zhang,Yuntian Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MV-PVMapper：免训练的多视角光伏组件建图框架，耦合视觉基础模型与无人机摄影测量，在屋顶电站实现近乎完整、几何精确的组件实例恢复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高保真光伏组件制图需完整实例恢复与准确边界刻画，而跨视角预测的不完整与不一致使“零样本分割”难以直接转为逐组件、几何精确的地图；同时希望避免任务特定标注与模型权重更新。

Method: 免训练框架MV-PVMapper：①SAM 3依据文本提示在原始影像生成候选；②经逐组件坡度改正将轮廓转移到物方，抑制表面模型噪声导致的畸变，并用代表性组件尺寸校验以指导跨视角实例选择；③将接受的实例回投至所有覆盖影像作为框提示，与文本提示共同驱动SAM 3迭代“分割—验证—融合”；约1 cm下视GSD、五方向航测，经两轮反馈收敛。

Result: 三座运营屋顶电站：两轮反馈在IoU=0.5匹配阈值下恢复7710/7711个参考组件且无误检；宏平均面积精确率99.00%、召回率98.52%；平均聚合Jaccard指数97.55%（正射影像分割基线60.73%、多视角像素投票基线41.24%）；IoU=0.95下平均精确率95.67%。消融表明组件尺寸约束对实例分离最关键，坡度改正与全覆盖视角反馈提升边界精度。

Conclusion: 多视角物方几何约束+基础模型提示反馈可显著优于正射分割与像素投票，且无需任务特定标注或分割模型权重更新，具备实用化建图潜力。

Abstract: High-fidelity photovoltaic (PV) module mapping requires complete instance recovery and accurate delineation. Vision foundation models offer zero-shot segmentation, but converting incomplete and inconsistent predictions across views into a complete, geometrically accurate map of individual modules remains challenging. We propose the Multi-View PV Module Mapper (MV-PVMapper), a training-free framework coupling vision foundation models with unmanned aerial vehicle (UAV) photogrammetry. First, Segment Anything Model 3 (SAM 3) generates candidates from original images using text prompts. Their contours are then transferred into object space through per-module slope correction to limit distortion from surface-model noise, and verified against representative module dimensions to guide cross-view instance selection. Finally, accepted instances return to all covering images as box prompts for SAM~3, supplementing text prompts with spatial cues for iterative segmentation, verification and fusion. On five-direction surveys of three operational rooftop PV plants at approximately 1 cm nadir ground sampling distance, two feedback iterations recover 7,710 of 7,711 reference modules with no false positives at an intersection-over-union (IoU) matching threshold of 0.5. Macro-averaged area precision and recall reach 99.00 % and 98.52 %, respectively. Averaged across sites, the aggregated Jaccard index reaches 97.55 %, compared with 60.73 % and 41.24 % for orthomosaic segmentation and multi-view pixel-voting baselines, respectively; average precision at the stricter IoU threshold of 0.95 reaches 95.67 %. Ablation studies show that module-size constraints are critical for instance separation, while slope correction and feedback to all covering views improve boundary accuracy. These results are achieved without task-specific image annotation or segmentation-model weight updates.

</details>


### [121] [When Cell Crops Share a Parent: Measuring and Isolating Acquisition Leakage in Cervical Cytology Benchmarks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05145)
*Snehasish Ghosh,Soham Mitra,Antareep Majumdar,KC Santosh,Nibaran Das*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 公开宫颈细胞学基准以"细胞裁剪块"(crop)为单位发布与划分数据，导致测试细胞与训练细胞共享同一采集母体(视野/玻片)，产生数据泄漏。预注册实验表明crop级划分会人为抬高准确率：SIPaKMeD +0.97pp、CRIC +12.08pp；增益主要来自"同源兄弟姐妹暴露"而非母体多样性，且解码性与显著性图均无法替代parent-disjoint划分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有宫颈细胞学基准通常从远少于细胞数量的显微镜视野或玻片中裁出大量细胞块，并按crop级随机划分训练/测试集，使测试细胞与训练细胞共享采集母体（视野、玻片、染色批次等），从而可能高估模型泛化能力。作者希望量化这一泄漏的幅度、归因其来源，并检验常用诊断手段（可解码性、Grad-CAM）能否识别泄漏。

Method: 预注册研究。在SIPaKMeD与CRIC两个基准上，跨12种架构、20个随机种子比较crop级划分与parent-disjoint划分的准确率差，并给出95%置信区间。在SIPaKMeD上设计析因实验，分别固定训练crop数与母体数，以分离"同源兄弟姐妹暴露"与"母体多样性"两种解释。进一步做三项诊断：以线性探针检验母体身份可解码性；通过擦除82%高于基线的线性可恢复性做消融；用Grad-CAM比较泄漏模型与干净模型；并将测试细胞颜色统计匹配到未见过的同类视野以检验颜色捷径。

Result: crop级划分使SIPaKMeD准确率提升0.97个百分点（95% CI 0.59–1.39），CRIC提升12.08个百分点（11.16–12.99）。析因实验显示增益主要由同源兄弟姐妹暴露贡献（1.73pp，0.95–2.51），而crop级划分带来的额外母体多样性贡献为0.00。母体身份可解码性约为随机水平的100倍，但擦除82%的高于基线线性可恢复性后准确率差距不变；Grad-CAM无法区分泄漏模型与干净模型；将测试细胞颜色统计匹配到未见同类视野后，大部分优势消失。

Conclusion: crop级划分在宫颈细胞学基准上造成实质性的乐观偏差，其机制是测试细胞与训练细胞共享采集母体的"兄弟姐妹"关系，并叠加颜色统计等采集级捷径，而非母体多样性不足。母体身份的可解码性与显著性图解释性都不能作为泄漏的有效代理指标。作者主张采用parent-disjoint（按视野/玻片/受试者划分）的切分协议，且该结论对同类单细胞衍生基准具有普适警示意义。

Abstract: Public cervical cytology benchmarks release many cell crops cut from far fewer microscope fields or slides and are often split at the crop level, so test cells share acquisition parents with training cells. In a preregistered study, crop-level splitting raised SIPaKMeD accuracy by 0.97 percentage points across twelve architectures and twenty seeds (95% CI 0.59-1.39), and CRIC accuracy by 12.08 points (11.16-12.99). On SIPaKMeD, a factorial that fixes training crop and parent counts attributes the gain to sibling exposure (1.73 points, 0.95-2.51), not to the extra parent diversity of crop-level splits (0.00). Parent identity is decodable at about 100 times chance, yet erasing 82% of its above-floor linear recoverability left the gap unchanged, and Grad-CAM did not distinguish leaking from clean models; matching test cells' color statistics to an unseen same-class field removed most of the advantage. Decodability and saliency cannot substitute for a parent-disjoint split.

</details>


### [122] [Perturb, Repair, Verify: Self-Play Vision-Language Verifiers for Compositional Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05158)
*Zijun Huang,Yingjun Du,Cees G. M. Snoek*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PROVE：一种通过“扰动-验证-修复-再验证”闭环自博弈训练视觉语言验证器的框架，摆脱对外部强评判器或人工难负样本的静态监督依赖。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉语言验证器的训练依赖更强的外部评判器或精心构造的难负样本，需要额外生成或标注、受教师模型上限约束，且难以随模型失败模式的演化而自适应调整。

Method: 以已知正确的图文对为起点，Mutator 对物体、属性、数量、关系施加带类型的扰动；验证器判定被扰动描述并给出文本反馈；Repairer 据此恢复忠实描述并进行二次验证。两轮验证产生结构化奖励，分别奖励有信息量的不匹配与视觉上可溯源的修复。扰动、验证、修复均为同一多模态骨干的角色条件行为，共享参数更新同时提升验证器本身。

Result: 在多种模型规模与模型族上提升细粒度组合式验证能力；在参考骨干上增强视觉细节鲁棒性；在更广泛的多模态任务上变化有限且依赖具体基准。

Conclusion: 自博弈式闭环训练可在无外部强监督下有效提升视觉语言验证器的组合式细粒度判别能力，但对通用多模态能力的迁移收益有限。

Abstract: Vision-language verifiers judge whether an image faithfully matches a textual description, yet existing training pipelines typically rely on static supervision from stronger external judges or curated hard-negative data. Such supervision requires external generation or annotation, is teacher-bounded, and is difficult to adapt as model failures evolve. We introduce PROVE (Perturb-and-Repair Optimization for Vision-Language Evaluation), a self-play framework that trains verifiers through a closed perturb-verify-repair-verify loop. Starting from a known-correct image-caption pair, a Mutator introduces a typed perturbation over objects, attributes, counts, or relations. The verifier then judges the perturbed caption and provides textual feedback, which a Repairer uses to recover a faithful caption for re-verification. These two verification rounds produce structured rewards for informative mismatches and visually grounded repairs. Since perturbation, verification, and repair are role-conditioned behaviors of the same evolving multimodal backbone, updating the shared parameters also improves the verifier itself. Experiments show that PROVE improves fine-grained compositional verification across model scales and families and strengthens visual-detail robustness on the reference backbone, with limited, benchmark-dependent changes on broader multimodal tasks.

</details>


### [123] [PadLoc: Training-Free Floor-Plan Localization by Sparse Geometric Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05172)
*Marian Bookhahn,Frank Neumann*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PadLoc 是一种免训练、传感器可插拔的基于平面图的定位后端，用稀疏几何匹配+高斯混合贝叶斯滤波替代密集位姿网格，取得更优或相当的精度且状态维度不随轨迹增长。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 平面图廉价、持久且对外观变化鲁棒，但现有学习深度方法依赖逐帧深度在密集 (x,y,θ) 网格上滤波，计算与内存开销大、状态随轨迹增长。作者希望保留可替换的深度前端，将后端替换为更高效的稀疏几何匹配。

Method: PadLoc 由三部分构成：(1) 主导墙面朝向生成小型位姿候选集合；(2) 用掩膜归一化互相关（masked NCC）将观测到的自由空间与平面图比对打分；(3) 采用移动位姿高斯混合贝叶斯滤波并结合一致性读出来解码轨迹。整体无需训练，前端深度模型可替换。

Result: 固定各对比方法的深度前端时，PadLoc 在 Gibson 各轨迹长度上持平或优于 UnLoc 前向滤波与 F3Loc，并且从 T=35 起超过 UnLoc 的离线优化；与 MapAnything 共享前端时优于 Z-FLoc 已发表结果；Bonn LiLocBench 上 SR@1m 较 UnLoc 提升 23–32pp（轮式里程计）与 35–43pp（真值运动），全视场 LiDAR+轮式里程计在静态序列达 100% SR@1m、中位误差 9–10cm；Hilti-Trimble 挑战赛中仅摄像头鱼眼装置在数百米施工楼层上三个会话达 0.90–0.98 在线 SR@2m，加入学习式墙体隔离后五个会话全覆盖（0.67–0.92），定位器保持不变。

Conclusion: PadLoc 证明稀疏几何匹配式后端可匹配或超越密集网格后端，同时保持传感器模块化与紧凑状态（不随轨迹长度增长），是一种高效、免训练的平面图定位方案。

Abstract: We propose PadLoc, a training-free localization back-end that places a moving sensor within a known floor plan. Floor plans are cheap, persistent, and robust to appearance change, but recent learned-depth methods localize against them by filtering per-frame depth through dense $(x,y,θ)$ pose grids. PadLoc keeps the swappable depth front-end and replaces the dense grid back-end with sparse geometric matching: dominant wall orientations define a small pose-candidate set, masked normalized cross-correlation scores observed free space against the plan, and a moving-pose Gaussian-mixture Bayes filter plus consensus readout decodes the trajectory. Holding each competitor's depth fixed, PadLoc matches or beats UnLoc's forward filter and the F3Loc filter at every Gibson trajectory length, and UnLoc's offline refinement from $T=35$ on; it exceeds Z-FLoc's published results with a shared MapAnything front-end. On Bonn LiLocBench, PadLoc improves pooled SR@1m over UnLoc by 23-32 pp under wheel odometry and 35-43 pp with ground-truth motion; with full-FOV LiDAR plus wheel odometry the same readout reaches 100% SR@1m at 9-10 cm median error on the static sequence. On the Hilti-Trimble Localization Challenge PadLoc localizes a camera-only fisheye rig on construction floors hundreds of metres across at 0.90-0.98 online SR@2m on three of five sessions; adding learned wall isolation, applied to every session, covers all five at 0.67-0.92 with the localizer unchanged throughout. PadLoc thus matches or outperforms dense grid back-ends while preserving sensor modularity and a compact state that does not grow with trajectory length.

</details>


### [124] [What Does the Model See? Representation Validity in Multimodal LLM-Based Video Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05181)
*Christian Pipal,Eva-Maria Vogel,Morgan Wack*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出"表征效度"(representation validity)概念，比较文本、音频、视频及组合输入在多模态大模型视频分析中对测量与后续统计估计的影响，发现没有任何输入表征始终最优，且分类准确率更高未必带来更接近人类编码的统计估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 社会科学家用MLLM大规模分析视频时，必须先决定把视频的哪些部分（转写文本、音频、原始视频或组合）作为输入。已有研究缺乏对输入表征如何影响测量结果及下游分析的系统的有效性检验，因此作者引入"表征效度"来填补这一空白。

Method: 在6个开源权重MLLM与3个人类标注视频数据集上比较不同输入表征（文本、音频、原生视频、组合）。在7个新闻相关构念上比较各模型的宏F1，并进一步检验不同表征所得标签复现人类编码回归系数与样本流行度估计的程度，共进行42组对比。

Result: 无任何表征一致最优：原生视频在7个构念上跨模型表现差异很小（宏F1=0.70–0.77），而提取文本表现从0.52到0.80不等；3个模型用文本更优、3个用视频更优。组合输入很少带来提升。更高分类准确率并不能可靠产生更接近人类编码的估计——最高F1表征仅在42组对比中的17组最好地复现回归系数。即使每个模型使用其最高F1的可用表征，仍有45%的样本流行度估计与人类编码基准相差超过10个百分点。

Conclusion: 输入表征的选择必须针对两个层面分别验证：所测量的构念，以及由所得标签估计的下游统计量。分类准确率不足以作为表征选择的依据，研究者不能默认最准确的表征会产生最可信的分析结果。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) allow social scientists to analyze video at scale, but researchers must decide which parts of a video to provide as input. We use the term representation validity to examine how text, audio, video, and combined inputs affect measurement and subsequent analysis. We compare input representations across six open-weight MLLMs and three human-annotated video datasets. No representation consistently performs best. On seven news-related constructs, native-video performance varies little across models (macro-F1 = .70-.77), while extracted-text performance ranges from .52 to .80. Three models perform better with text and three with video. Combining inputs rarely improves performance. Higher classification accuracy also does not reliably produce estimates closer to those based on human codes. The highest-F1 representation best reproduces human-coded regression coefficients in 17 of 42 comparisons. Even when each model uses its highest-F1 deployable representation, 45% of sample-prevalence estimates differ from the human-coded benchmark by more than ten percentage points. Input representation therefore requires validation for both the construct being measured and the quantity estimated from the resulting labels.

</details>


### [125] [From Natural Images to Medical Scans: Transferability of Diffusion Models for Medical Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05192)
*Efthymios Georgiou,Ioannis Kakogeorgiou,Sarah Brüningk*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 系统评估自然图像预训练向医学扩散去噪器迁移，发现迁移显著提升生成质量，其收益来自空间结构而非语义，并与下游任务表现一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医学扩散模型多从头训练去噪器，自然图像迁移在生成建模（尤其扩散去噪器）中的作用缺乏系统评估，作者希望厘清迁移的收益大小、来源机制及适用条件。

Method: 在潜空间与像素空间两种范式、131M–952M 多种模型规模、两个放射数据集（RadImageNet、BraTS）上开展对照实验；用语义探测与适配去噪器内部表征的空间结构指标作机制分析；并比较混合域训练与多种重初始化方案。

Result: i) 自然图像初始化稳定提升生成质量，像素空间 gFID 相对改善 21–34%，潜空间约 14%（tokeniser 已提供较多先验）；gFID 增益与下游病理分类及放射组学特征 FD 一致。ii) 收益源于空间结构而非语义：语义探测无法预测生成质量，而基于内部表征的空间结构指标与 gFID 相关达 |r|=0.85。iii) 自然与医学图像在晚期 block 之前的层级表征相似，混合自然图像训练可提升保真度。iv) 收益依赖自然图像初始化，优于域内医学预训练及多种重初始化方案。

Conclusion: 在该设定下自然图像去噪器可正向迁移至医学去噪器；迁移价值主要由空间组织这一通道承载，且像素空间比潜空间获益更大。

Abstract: Transfer learning from natural images is standard in supervised and increasingly adopted in self-supervised medical image analysis, but its role in generative modelling, and in particular diffusion denoisers, remains underexplored. Medical diffusion models typically train the denoiser from scratch, with no systematic evaluation of natural-image transfer. We analyse this across latent and pixel paradigms, model sizes (131M-952M) and two radiology datasets (RadImageNet, BraTS), and ask which aspects of transfer support generation. We find that: i) Natural-image initialisation consistently improves generation, by 21-34% relative gFID in pixel space against 14% in latent space, where the tokeniser supplies much of the prior. gFID gains mirror downstream pathology classification and radiomic-feature FD, both proxies for clinical fidelity. ii) Benefits reflect spatial structure, not semantics: semantic probing does not predict generation quality, whereas a spatial-structure metric adapted to internal denoiser representations tracks gFID at $|r|=0.85$, positioning spatial organisation as a channel through which pretraining manifests. iii) Mixed-domain denoiser training shows that natural and medical images share similar layer-wise representations up to the late blocks, and mixing natural images into medical training improves fidelity. iv) Transfer benefit is tied to natural image initialisation, outperforming in-domain medical pretraining and several reinitialisation schemes. Overall, natural image denoisers transfer positively to medical ones in our setup. Code is available at https://github.com/CAIROLab-Bern/rad-dit

</details>


### [126] [Factorized MLLM Embeddings for Composed Image Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05193)
*WonJun Moon,Seongchan Kim,Dahun Kim,Seungryong Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 FactEmbed，将组合图像检索中的“组合语义理解”与“判别性嵌入构建”解耦：用一组因子化隐变量经由“目标图像想象”中间目标显式模拟预期视觉修改，再以因子级对比损失并蒸馏到 MLLM 深层注意力层，推理时用 [EOS] 全局嵌入加因子隐变量均值池化重排，兼顾精度与效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于 MLLM 的 CIR 方法仅用对比监督把组合查询直接映射为单一检索嵌入，未显式解析参考图中需要发生哪些视觉修改，导致 MLLM 的组合理解能力被严重低估、未被充分利用。

Method: 1) 引入因子化隐变量集合，通过中间“目标图像想象”目标让隐变量在形成判别表示前先落地到目标语义；2) 采用因子级对比损失，并将分解表示蒸馏至 MLLM 更深的注意力层，使其在模型中传播；3) 推理时保留 [EOS] token 构建全局嵌入，同时利用同一次前向得到的均值池化因子隐变量对短候选列表低成本重排。

Result: 在多个 CIR 基准上的大量实验表明，FactEmbed 优于既有方法。

Conclusion: 显式建模组合视觉修改、将理解与嵌入构建解耦，是释放 MLLM 在组合图像检索中潜力的有效途径；单次前向产出双粒度表示可在几乎零额外开销下进一步提升检索效果。

Abstract: Composed Image Retrieval (CIR) aims to retrieve a target image given a reference image and a modification text. Although recent approaches leveraging Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) have shown significant progress, we argue that the MLLM's compositional understanding remains underutilized. This stems from directly mapping composed queries into a retrieval embedding via contrastive supervision alone, without explicitly resolving the intended visual modifications. In this work, we introduce Factorized MLLM Embeddings (FactEmbed), which decouples compositional query comprehension from building discriminative embeddings. Rather than directly projecting the composed query into a single embedding without internal reasoning, FactEmbed employs a set of factorized latents that simulate the intended visual modifications via an intermediate target imagination objective. This process encourages the latents to ground into target semantics before forming discriminative representations for retrieval. Subsequently, to propagate these factorized representations throughout the model for retrieval, we optimize a factor-level contrastive loss while distilling the decomposed representations into a deeper attention layer of the MLLM. Finally, for efficient inference, we retain the [EOS] token to establish a global-level embedding on top of these factorized latents, while we exploit the mean-pooled factorized latents to rerank a short candidate list at negligible cost, as both are produced by the same forward pass. Extensive evaluations demonstrate that FactEmbed outperforms prior works across CIR benchmarks.

</details>


### [127] [Robust Reconstruction of Power Lines from Incomplete Mobile and Aerial LiDAR Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05197)
*Mohamed Gaha,Damos Ayobo Abongo,Philippe Massicotte,Eya Ben Kacem,Wael Jaafar,Rami Langar,Christian Buteau*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种结合尺度化密度聚类、鲁棒多项式拟合与迭代片段合并的自动化电力线重建方法，可统一处理机载与移动LiDAR点云，在严重缺失与遮挡下恢复单根导线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 配电网络植被侵入评估与风险缓解需准确定位并区分中压、低压、中性线及通信线。LiDAR点云虽提供三维信息，但导线回波稀疏、不完整、含噪且被植被遮挡；配电网络杆塔矮、线型复杂，导线紧邻树木，问题更突出；且移动与机载LiDAR在点密度、视角几何、覆盖范围与遮挡模式上差异显著，需统一的鲁棒方法。

Method: 在语义分类之后，采用统一的自动化重建流程：尺度化密度聚类（scaled density-based clustering）分离候选点，鲁棒多项式拟合（robust polynomial fitting）拟合线形，迭代片段合并（iterative fragment merging）恢复被遮挡或断裂的单根导线；同一流程无需模态专用代码或参数调优即可处理两类数据。评估包括退化实验、基准对比、参数敏感性分析与大规模验证。

Result: 机载数据集：精确率96.87%、召回率96.66%、F1 96.76%、几何RMSE 0.127 m；移动数据集（无导线级参考标注）：点到模型RMSE 0.045 m，重建覆盖率95.27%。代码、文档与评估数据已公开。

Conclusion: 所提方法在严重数据缺失与遮挡条件下稳健重建配电线路导线，且以单一无调参流程兼容机载与移动LiDAR，具备实用性与可复现性。

Abstract: Accurate vegetation-encroachment assessment and risk mitigation require electric utilities to accurately locate and characterize distribution-network assets, including poles and power lines, and distinguish among medium-voltage, low-voltage, neutral, and telecommunication lines. Mobile and aerial Light Detection and Ranging (LiDAR) point clouds provide detailed 3D representations of these assets and their surroundings. However, power-line detection and reconstruction remain challenging because conductor returns are often sparse, incomplete, noisy, or occluded by vegetation. These challenges are especially significant in distribution networks, where lower pole heights and complex line configurations place conductors close to trees. In addition, mobile and aerial LiDAR datasets differ substantially in point density, viewing geometry, spatial coverage, and occlusion patterns. We propose a robust and automated reconstruction method combining scaled density-based clustering, robust polynomial fitting, and iterative fragment merging to recover individual conductors under severe data incompleteness and occlusion. Following semantic classification, the same reconstruction pipeline processes both mobile and aerial LiDAR data without modality-specific code or parameter tuning. The method is evaluated through degradation experiments, benchmarking, parameter sensitivity analysis, and large-scale validation. On the aerial dataset, it achieves 96.87% precision, 96.66% recall, a 96.76% F$_1$-score, and a geometric RMSE of 0.127 m. On the mobile dataset, where conductor-level reference annotations were unavailable, it achieves a point-to-model RMSE of 0.045 m and a reconstruction coverage of 95.27%. Source code, documentation, and evaluation data are publicly available at https://github.com/varen-project.

</details>


### [128] [TAQ-MOTR: Tracking-Aware Detection Query Formation for End-to-End Multiple Object Tracking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05205)
*Hanyang Liu,Hong Zhang,Ding Yuan,Yang Han,Yifan Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出TAQ-MOTR，用轨迹状态条件化的检测查询生成（HQG）+训练期辅助检测分支，改进TBP类多目标跟踪的检测查询形成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: TBP方法中检测与关联紧耦合，检测查询需承担两项职责：发现当前未跟踪目标，以及为被保留但难跟踪（遮挡、可见性下降）的轨迹提供互补的当帧证据。现有方法沿用单帧检测的查询构造方式，在解码器前构建查询且不感知轨迹状态，导致漏检未跟踪目标、难轨迹缺乏互补证据，进而关联失败或产生重复响应。

Method: 提出tracking-aware检测查询形成框架TAQ-MOTR：1）解码器前的混合查询生成器HQG，融合当前帧图像特征、track memory与可学习memory，使检测查询以轨迹状态为条件，适配未跟踪目标发现与已跟踪目标补充两类用途；2）仅训练期使用的辅助检测分支，其一对一标签分配与主跟踪分支解耦，为共享的HQG与解码器提供额外检测监督。

Result: 在DanceTrack测试集上达到72.0 HOTA、63.1 AssA、76.6 IDF1；并在DanceTrack、SportsMOT、BFT上整体性能提升。

Conclusion: 通过将检测查询的构造与轨迹状态耦合，并辅以解耦监督的训练期检测分支，可在保持端到端TBP框架无启发式后处理优势的同时，兼顾新目标发现与难轨迹补充证据，提升检测-关联一体化跟踪性能。

Abstract: Tracking-by-propagation (TBP) methods perform end-to-end multi-object tracking by propagating track queries across frames, jointly handling detection and identity association without heuristic post-processing while naturally modeling temporal information. However, as detection and association are tightly coupled, detection queries in TBP are expected to discover currently untracked objects and provide complementary current-frame evidence for challenging tracks whose queries and identities are retained. Existing methods largely inherit detection-query formation from single-frame detection and construct such queries before the decoder with little awareness of trajectory states. Consequently, currently untracked objects may be missed, while challenging tracked objects with reduced visibility under occlusion may lack complementary evidence, causing association failures or duplicate responses. To address these issues, we propose TAQ-MOTR, a tracking-aware detection query formation framework that employs a pre-decoder Hybrid Query Generator (HQG) to fuse current-frame image features, track memory, and learnable memory. By conditioning detection queries on trajectory states, TAQ-MOTR adapts them to \rev{the discovery of currently untracked objects and tracked-object complementation We further introduce a training-only auxiliary detection branch whose one-to-one label assignment is decoupled from that of the main tracking branch, providing additional detection supervision to strengthen the shared HQG and decoder for the discovery of currently untracked objects. TAQ-MOTR improves performance on DanceTrack, SportsMOT, and BFT, achieving 72.0 HOTA, 63.1 AssA, and 76.6 IDF1 on the DanceTrack test set. Code is available at https://github.com/Magicmiya/TAQ-MOTR

</details>


### [129] [Diffusion Plug-in Priors for Self-Supervised Ultrasound Video Restoration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05206)
*Chu Chen,Mingduo He,Yining Li,Yinhao Wu,Wei Tang,Yiwen Luo,Yixuan Chen,Graham Treece,Pasquale Cascarano,Kangning Cui*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: UltraDiff：以冻结预训练扩散模型为即插即用先验，通过优化采样器潜在种子实现无配对数据的超声视频联合超分与去斑。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超声视频受硬件限制的空间分辨率与信号相关散斑（speckle）制约，而有监督复原所需的低/高分辨率配对采集几乎不可得。

Method: 冻结扩散模型作为先验，逐帧在range-constrained MAP框架下优化扩散采样器的latent种子；由Rayleigh尺度族模型导出散斑感知数据保真项，并分析其相对残差与方差自适应加权特性；提出在log强度域下（无clamp形式）有下界且严格凸的稳定化保真项；结合全变分（TV）正则；用前一帧优化种子热启动实现时间种子更新。

Result: EchoNet-LVH模拟散斑与加性噪声下，自然图像先验较最强基线PSNR提升1.24 dB，超声相关先验进一步提升；真实临床超声心动图视频上，零样本EF预测MAE由基线DUP的7.31降至6.37 EF点。

Conclusion: 散斑感知保真项与冻结扩散先验结合，可实现无需真值的超声视频复原，并有利于下游测量任务。

Abstract: \textit{Objective:} Ultrasound videos are limited by hardware-constrained spatial resolution and signal-dependent speckle, while paired low- and high-resolution acquisitions for supervised restoration are rarely available. We aim to jointly super-resolve and despeckle ultrasound videos without paired training data or network retraining. \textit{Methods:} We propose UltraDiff, which uses a frozen pre-trained diffusion model (DM) as a plug-in prior and restores each frame by optimizing the latent seed of the DM sampler under a range-constrained maximum a posteriori formulation. We derive a speckle-aware data-fidelity term from a Rayleigh scale-family model and characterize its relative-residual and variance-adaptive weighting properties. We further introduce a stabilized fidelity that is bounded below and strictly convex in log intensity for positive observations in its unclamped form. A total-variation regularizer complements the DM prior, and a temporal seed update warm-starts each frame from the optimized seed of its predecessor. \textit{Results:} On EchoNet-LVH videos with simulated speckle and additive noise, UltraDiff with a natural-image prior improves PSNR by 1.24\,dB over the strongest baseline, and ultrasound-related priors improve it further. On real clinical echocardiography videos, UltraDiff reduces the mean absolute error of zero-shot ejection fraction (EF) prediction from 7.31 to 6.37 EF points relative to DUP, the strongest baseline. \textit{Conclusion:} Combining a speckle-aware fidelity with frozen diffusion priors enables ground-truth-free ultrasound video restoration that benefits downstream measurement. \textit{Significance:} UltraDiff needs neither paired data nor retraining and is not tied to a specific diffusion checkpoint. Code associated with this work will be made publicly available upon acceptance.

</details>


### [130] [PLANTEUR: A Crop-Type Segmentation Benchmark for Tropical Regions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05223)
*Piper Wolters,Gabriel Tseng,Favyen Bastani,Joseph Redmon,Yawen Zhang,Hadrien Sablon,Ando Shah*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PLANTEUR 是一个面向法国海外领地热带作物类型制图的卫星影像基准数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有作物类型制图基准多集中于欧美温带地区（如 PASTIS），缺乏热带多岛屿、跨区域泛化场景的评测资源。

Method: 基于 PASTIS 设计，构建地理配准并对齐的 Sentinel-2、Sentinel-1 与 Landsat-8 时序影像，配以地块级标签，覆盖6个热带区域、8类标签空间；设定标准测试划分与"留一岛屿"（leave-one-island-out）协议以评估跨领地泛化，并评测全监督模型、线性探针与微调基础模型。

Result: 提供多源多时相数据与两套评测协议，支持对不同建模范式（全监督、线性探针、微调基础模型）的系统比较。

Conclusion: PLANTEUR 以开源方式（GitHub、Hugging Face）发布代码与数据，为热带跨域作物制图研究提供标准化基准。

Abstract: We introduce PLANTEUR, a benchmark for crop-type mapping from satellite images in the French overseas territories. Building on the PASTIS design, PLANTEUR provides georeferenced and aligned Sentinel-2, Sentinel-1, and Landsat-8 sequences with parcel-derived labels spanning six tropical regions and an 8-class label space. We define both a standard test split and a leave-one-island-out protocol to measure cross-territory generalization, and evaluate fully supervised models, linear probes, and fine-tuned foundation models. The benchmark code and data are open source and available on GitHub and Hugging Face.

</details>


### [131] [Diagnosis by Composition: Detecting and Localizing Unseen Diseases by Composing Learned Radiological Findings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05231)
*Kaihua Hou,Wei Liu,Yi Huang,Hao Zhang,Haibo Jin,Yifei Sun,Yuan gao,Zuoou Li,Ling Zhang,Ahmed Alaa,Geoffrey H. Tison,Xu Han,Ke Yan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出"诊断即组合"(DbC)表征学习框架，学习放射学征象词表并将疾病定义为征象图的无参固定组合，实现可解释、可审计、可零训练扩展与诊断未见疾病的影像诊断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有放射影像深度模型直接从图像预测疾病标签，受限于训练时见过的疾病类别，且决策难以用放射科医生的语言（征象）解释。而临床放射学实际遵循"视觉征象→依据指南→疾病"的显式、可复现决策逻辑，作者希望将这一"征象优先"范式编码进表征学习。

Method: DbC 将图像特征与放射学征象的文本嵌入对齐，学习一个征象词表，每个征象表示为扫描上的稠密图（dense map）。疾病由其指南导出的必需征象集合定义，疾病诊断图为对应征象图的固定、无参数（parameter-free）组合。因征象→疾病映射来自指南而非学习得到，故天然可解释、可审计，新增疾病只需指定其征象而无需重训练，并可通过组合征象诊断未见疾病。

Result: 在多期肝脏 CT 上，DbC 对未见疾病、来自不同机构的罕见疾病以及外部队列的检测与定位均显著优于所有对比方法，包括图像与疾病名称对齐的模型；在完全监督设置下也提升了检测与定位性能，并在不同训练集规模下保持优势。

Conclusion: DbC 以征象为中心的指南式组合推理在肝脏 CT 上验证有效，凡放射学指南以征象定义疾病的场景均可推广；其零重训练扩展与未见疾病诊断能力为可解释、可审计的临床影像 AI 提供了新范式。

Abstract: Deep models for radiological diagnosis are typically trained to predict disease labels directly from images, which limits them to diseases seen during training and makes their decisions hard to interpret in radiologists' terms. Radiology, however, proceeds from visual findings to disease through published guidelines that make this decision rule explicit, interpretable, and reproducible. We introduce diagnosis by composition (DbC), a representation learning framework that encodes this findings-first logic. DbC learns a vocabulary of radiological findings by aligning image features with the text embeddings of the findings, each finding represented as a dense map over the scan. A disease is defined by a set of required findings derived from its guidelines, and its diagnosis map is a fixed, parameter-free composition of the corresponding finding maps. Because the finding-to-disease mapping is derived from guidelines rather than learned, DbC is interpretable and auditable by design, can extend to new diseases by specifying their findings without retraining, and can diagnose unseen diseases by composing their findings. On multi-phase liver CT, DbC detects and localizes unseen diseases, rare diseases from a different institution, and an external cohort significantly better than all comparators, including models that align images with disease names. DbC also improves detection and localization in the fully supervised setting and remains superior across training-set sizes. Although demonstrated on liver CT, DbC applies wherever radiological guidelines define diseases through findings.

</details>


### [132] [Route Anything: A Study of Model and Policy Routing for General-Purpose AI Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05275)
*Pengfei Zhou,Yixing Ma,Hexin Wang,Weidong Tang,Zhenglin Wan,Wangbo Zhao,Yang You*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 Route Anything 统一框架，研究跨多模态、多能力专用模型（候选）的动态路由，建立质量—成本权衡下的最优路由理论，并构建覆盖25个基准、374,401条评分记录、452个候选配置的全模态轨迹矩阵，训练并评估路由器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通向 AGI 的路径可能不是单一模型掌握全部能力，而是将异构的专用模型、工具与策略编排为统一系统；核心问题是路由器如何在性能与计算成本之间权衡，动态选择并协调最合适的候选。

Method: 1) 建立统一理论：刻画质量—成本权衡下的最优路由，并说明路由器可用信息如何决定其可达性能；2) 构建全模态轨迹矩阵，覆盖语言理解、图像/视频/语音生成、编程与办公智能体、机器人操作等25个基准，含452个候选配置的374,401条评分运行；3) 在此框架上训练并测试多种路由器，分析全模态路由潜力、学习型路由器能力及效率提升条件。

Result: 逐输入 oracle 路由比最佳固定候选高5.4分；学习型路由器在16个基准上高0.3分、视频生成高2.1分、机器人操作高1.1分，同时策略用时减少24%；路由相对参考模型平均提升效率8.82倍。

Conclusion: 路由存在显著的能力编排空间，学习型路由器可在多模态与智能体任务中稳定超越最佳单一候选并显著降低计算成本；信息可得性是决定路由性能的关键因素。

Abstract: Artificial general intelligence (AGI) requires a method that can understand, generate, reason, and act across diverse domains and modalities. Rather than relying on a single model to master every capability, a promising path toward AGI now is to orchestrate a heterogeneous ecosystem of specialized models, tools, and policies (candidates) into a unified AI system. At the heart of this vision lies a fundamental question: how can an AI system dynamically select and coordinate the most capable candidate for each task while balancing performance and computational cost? In this work, we present Route Anything, a unified framework for studying model and policy routing across general-purpose AI systems. We develop a unified theoretical foundation that characterizes optimal routing under quality--cost trade-offs and establishes how the information available to a router determines its attainable performance. To systematically investigate routing across diverse capabilities, we first construct an omni-modal trajectory matrix spanning 25 benchmarks across language understanding, image, video, and speech generation, coding and workplace agents, and robotic manipulation, comprising 374,401 scored runs from 452 candidate configurations. Building on this framework, we train and test different routers to investigate the potential of omni-modal model routing, the capabilities of learned routers, and the conditions under which routing improves efficiency. Our results reveal substantial opportunities for capability orchestration: per-input oracle routing outperforms the best fixed candidate by 5.4 points, and learned routers outperform it by 0.3 points across 16 benchmarks, by 2.1 points on video generation, and by 1.1 points on robot manipulation while using 24% less policy time. More importantly, routing raises efficiency by 8.82x on average compared with the reference model.

</details>


### [133] [Möbius: A Unified Motion-Language Model Bridging Understanding and Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05279)
*Jiazhi Shu,Chengfeng Zhao,Jinyi Wang,Yubo Zhao,Zhiyang Dou,Ronghui Li,Yu Lu,Xinwei Jiang,Zijiao Zeng,Yuan Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Möbius 将扩散生成过程引入与运动理解共享的多模态表示空间，直接建模连续帧级原始运动，无需离散分词或预训练运动 VAE，在单一框架内统一运动理解、生成与编辑。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态运动模型（UMMs）仅部分统一：理解由语言模型承担，生成则委托给独立的扩散模块并作用于学习到的运动隐变量，导致理解与生成割裂。

Method: 将整个扩散过程置于与运动理解相同的多模态表示空间中；干净运动、含噪运动与文本共同参与共享注意力，同时用角色专属参数分别处理理解流与生成流；直接作用于连续帧级原始运动，不使用离散 token 化，也不需要预训练运动 VAE。

Result: 在 HumanML3D 上，于统一运动模型中取得 state-of-the-art；扩展至 1.7B 参数、1,700 小时运动数据后仍表现有效，验证了框架的可扩展性。

Conclusion: 用共享表示空间整合扩散生成与语言理解是一种简洁有效的统一运动建模范式，单一模型即可支持运动描述、文本到运动生成、时序补全与指令引导编辑，并具备良好缩放性。

Abstract: We aim to build a truly unified motion model that can understand, generate, and edit human motion within a single framework. Existing motion UMMs remain only partially unified: motion understanding is handled by a language model, while generation is often delegated to a separate diffusion module operating over learned motion latents. We introduce Möbius, which brings the entire diffusion process into the same multimodal representation space used for motion understanding. Clean motion, noisy motion, and text jointly participate in shared attention, while role-specific parameters process understanding and generation streams. Importantly, Möbius operates directly on continuous frame-level raw motion, requiring neither discrete tokenization nor a pretrained motion VAE. This simple formulation naturally supports motion captioning, text-to-motion generation, temporal completion, and instruction-guided editing within a single model. On HumanML3D, Möbius achieves state-of-the-art performance among unified motion models. Scaling to 1.7B parameters and 1,700 hours of motion data further demonstrates that our framework is both effective and scalable.

</details>


### [134] [YANAMI: A Large-Scale Multimodal Dataset and Benchmark for Multiturn Stateful Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05280)
*Shengyu Wang,Tengyue Nong,Junming Liu,Haosong Peng,Siyu Li,Ruiqi Lei,Fushuo Huo,Yuhao Pan,Yunfeng Fan,Ding Wang,Nan Cheng,Haozhao Wang,Wenchao Xu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出首个面向多模态有状态交互的大规模对话数据集与基准 YANAMI，包含 400K 多轮多模态对话（YANAMI-400K）与 400 组评测对话（YANAMI-Bench），要求模型跨轮理解、维护、更新并忠实视觉化呈现演化的对话状态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有统一多模态模型的数据集与基准以单轮任务为主；即便是近期多轮数据集，许多轮次可独立求解，无需跨轮追踪信息，因而对"多模态有状态交互"的监督与评测不足。

Method: 构建 YANAMI-400K：400K 多轮多模态对话、超 2M 图像、2.6M 对话轮次，覆盖多图融合、图像编辑、世界建模、视觉推理四类场景，要求跨轮持续追踪、保持与修订多模态对话状态；构建 YANAMI-Bench：400 组多轮对话，联合评测对话状态理解与视觉实现；并在 BAGEL 上进行微调验证。

Result: 在 YANAMI-400K 上微调 BAGEL 后，图像编辑、生成与多模态有状态交互分别提升 10.3%、2.0%、13.23%，且保持通用视觉理解能力；YANAMI-Bench 评测显示现有 UMM 在维护、更新及视觉实现演化对话状态方面仍存在明显困难。

Conclusion: 多模态有状态交互是当前 UMM 的关键缺口；YANAMI 提供了有效的训练与评测基础设施，且揭示"状态理解"与"视觉实现"之间存在差距，为后续统一多模态模型研究提供方向。

Abstract: Unified Multimodal Models (UMMs) integrate visual understanding, generation, and editing within a single unified framework. However, existing unified multimodal datasets and benchmarks remain dominated by single-turn tasks. Even in recent multi-turn datasets, many turns can be solved independently without tracking information across turns. Consequently, they provide limited supervision and evaluation for multimodal stateful interaction, which requires models to understand, maintain, update, and faithfully realize evolving dialogue states across turns. To address this gap, we introduce YANAMI, the first large-scale multimodal dialogue dataset and benchmark for stateful interaction, comprising YANAMI-400K and YANAMI-Bench. YANAMI-400K contains 400K multi-turn multimodal dialogues, with more than 2M images and 2.6M dialogue turns. The dialogues span four representative scenarios: Multi-Image Fusion, Image Editing, World Modeling, and Visual Reasoning, and require models to continuously track, preserve, and revise evolving multimodal dialogue states across turns. YANAMI-Bench comprises 400 multi-turn dialogues for systematically evaluating multimodal stateful interaction. It jointly evaluates Dialogue State Understanding and Visual Realization, characterizing the gap between models' ability to understand evolving dialogue states and their ability to faithfully realize them in visual outputs. Experiments show that fine-tuning BAGEL on YANAMI-400K improves image editing, generation, and multimodal stateful interaction by 10.3%, 2.0%, and 13.23%, respectively, while preserving its general visual understanding capabilities. Evaluations on YANAMI-Bench further reveal that existing UMMs still struggle to maintain, update, and visually realize evolving multimodal dialogue states.

</details>


### [135] [Anchored Rank-Adversarial Fine-Tuning Pretrained Visual Place Recognition Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05286)
*Mostafa Kira,Bader Rasheed,Manuel Mazzara*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出锚定排序对抗微调（ARAFT），在固定的干净精度预算下，通过基于最难正样本的列表排序损失与冻结预训练模型干净描述子的锚约束，显著提升VPR模型的对抗鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基础模型VPR（如SuperVLAD、BoQ）跨城市、季节、光照精度高，但在白盒攻击、1/255不可感知扰动下排序被破坏，R@1几乎归零，缺乏鲁棒性。

Method: ARAFT：对每个对抗查询的hardest positive施加listwise rank loss，并以冻结预训练模型的干净描述子作锚约束进行微调；checkpoint在预先固定的干净精度预算内选取，并完整报告精度-鲁棒性权衡曲线。

Result: 在干净R@1下降5个点预算内，普通对抗训练无可接受checkpoint，ARAFT将鲁棒R@1从0提升至SuperVLAD 47.3、BoQ 55.3。测试集上2/255时与无预算普通对抗训练鲁棒R@1相当且干净R@1高约10点；4/255时普通对抗训练鲁棒性领先6-9点但仍落后10点干净精度。

Conclusion: ARAFT在受限干净代价下实现有效鲁棒性提升，可从SuperVLAD无调参迁移至BoQ，对更强优化器仍鲁棒且无梯度掩蔽迹象。

Abstract: Visual place recognition (VPR) localises a camera by retrieving similar geo-tagged images, and current foundation-model VPR models are accurate across cities, seasons and illumination, but not robust to adversarial perturbations: under a white-box attack on the ranking at an imperceptible budget of 1/255, SuperVLAD and BoQ keep at most 0.2% top-1 recall (R@1) on four benchmarks. We propose anchored rank-adversarial fine-tuning (ARAFT), which fine-tunes a released model with a listwise rank loss on each adversarial query's hardest positive and an anchor to the clean descriptor of the frozen pretrained model. Checkpoints are selected under a clean-accuracy budget fixed in advance, and the full trade-off is reported. Within five validation R@1 points of clean cost, plain adversarial training has no admissible checkpoint, whereas ARAFT raises robust R@1 from zero to 47.3 on SuperVLAD and 55.3 on BoQ; plain adversarial training exceeds this only at about three times the clean cost. Against unbudgeted plain adversarial training on the test sets, ARAFT matches its robust R@1 at 2/255 with about ten more clean R@1 points. At 4/255, plain adversarial training is 6-9 robust R@1 points ahead, while ARAFT still keeps its ten-point clean advantage. Developed on SuperVLAD, ARAFT transfers to BoQ without re-tuning, and its robustness holds against stronger optimisers, with no sign of gradient masking.

</details>


### [136] [Low-Density 3D Point Cloud Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05291)
*Ahmed Baha Ben Jmaa,Faten Chaieb*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出LD-PointNet++，一种面向低密度3D点云分类的专用网络架构，并结合首个系统性密度基准测试，证明其在低密度场景下优于现有方法，且在多个密度水平上保持90%以上的准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3D点云分类方法在密集点云上表现优异，但受传感技术限制和环境条件变化影响，真实场景中点云密度往往较低且差异显著，导致这些方法性能明显下降。因此需要针对低密度点云分类问题开展系统性评估并提出适配架构。

Method: 首先构建首个基准测试研究，系统评估当前先进点云分类方法在不同密度水平下的表现；在此基础上设计LD-PointNet++架构，专门针对低密度点云的分类进行优化。

Result: 基准测试显示现有先进方法在低密度环境下性能显著下降；在ModelNet基准数据集上，LD-PointNet++在低密度场景中优于对比方法，并在不同密度水平下保持90%以上的一致准确率。

Conclusion: 密度变化是点云分类在真实应用中不可忽视的关键挑战，针对性设计的LD-PointNet++能够有效缓解低密度导致的性能退化，实现跨密度水平的稳健分类性能。

Abstract: 3D point cloud classification, a task in computer vision, has recently gained substantial interest due to its extensive applications across various domains such as augmented and virtual reality, robotics, and autonomous driving. However, classifying 3D point clouds becomes challenging in real-world scenarios where the density levels vary. These variations are due to limitations inherent to current sensing technologies and variable environmental conditions, which often prevent the capture of detailed and comprehensive geometric representations. Existing classification methods, which have demonstrated impressive results on dense point clouds, may be affected and could experience reduced performance when dealing with low-density point clouds. Addressing this challenge, this paper introduces LD-PointNet++, an architecture optimized for the classification of low-density point clouds. To pave the way for LD-PointNet++, we conduct the first benchmarking study that systematically evaluates state-of-the-art point cloud classification methods across varying densities, revealing significant performance declines in low-density environments. Experimental results on the ModelNet benchmark dataset demonstrate that LD-PointNet++ not only outperforms these methods in low-density scenarios but also demonstrates consistent accuracy above 90% across different density levels.

</details>


### [137] [Wonder 4: A Mixture-of-Experts Recipe for Image Quality Enhancement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05294)
*Yunan Wu,Bin Sun,Partha Acharjee,Xiaoyu Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出 Wonder 4，一种面向真实照片多重退化（模糊、噪点、压缩、划痕、低分辨率）的图像质量增强 MoE 模型：每个 block 含 13 个专家，路由器为每个 patch 选 5 个专家，无需退化分类器、掩码、提示或任务标签；并以"最小使用份额偏置"替代标准 MoE 的均衡使用约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实照片常同时含多种退化，各退化有不同严重程度与空间位置，且对应修复相互冲突；现有模型用单一全局描述表征整幅图像，无法指明各修复应作用于何处。

Method: MoE 架构：每 block 含 N=13 个专家，可学习路由器将每个图像 patch 路由到 k=5 个专家，端到端训练，无退化分类器/掩码/提示/检测器/任务标签。训练策略上用"最小使用份额"偏置替代语言模型式均等使用规则：全局使用率低于阈值的专家获得更高路由分数直至被再次选中，高于阈值的专家不受影响；该偏置无需额外损失、额外模块或处理步骤。

Result: 在 DIV2K 与 Flickr2K 上，与 5 个已发表方法和 3 个开源权重流程对比，在 DIV2K ×4、DIV2K ×8、Flickr2K ×4 三种设置下均取得最优 LPIPS、DISTS 和 NIQE。

Conclusion: 空间自适应的专家路由可在无显式退化先验的条件下同时处理多种缺陷并按区域施加修复；放宽的"最小使用份额"偏置优于强制均衡使用，是 MoE 图像复原训练的有效替代方案。

Abstract: A real photograph is often blurry, grainy, compressed, scratched, and low-resolution at the same time. Each defect has its own severity and its own place in the image. The corrections for these defects conflict with each other. Exist- ing models describe the whole image with one description, so they cannot say where each correction belongs. To solve this problem, we present Wonder 4, a mixture-of-experts (MoE) model for image quality enhancement. It corrects several defects of a real photograph at the same time, each in the region where it occurs. Every block of the model holds a mixture of N = 13 experts. A router, trained with the model, sends every image patch to k = 5 of them. No degradation classifier, mask, prompt, detector, or task label is used. Another issue is that standard MoE training, taken from language models, pushes every expert toward equal use. Equal use applies every correction everywhere, even where it is not needed. We replace this rule with a bias that only sets a minimum share of use. Across all patches, an expert used less than this minimum gets higher router scores until it is selected again. An expert above the minimum is not changed, so no expert is forced to be used as often as the others. The bias needs no extra loss. The method adds no extra module and no extra processing step. We evaluate Wonder 4 on DIV2K and Flickr2K, against five published methods and three open-weight pipelines.
